# Graph Report - Sixteen  (2026-10-20)

## Corpus Check
- 3595 files · ~3,191,450 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 662 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .css 399, .woff2 78, .lock 77)

## Summary
- 81199 nodes · 184169 edges · 3788 communities (2294 shown, 1494 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 83% EXTRACTED · 17% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 32070 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `29298e79`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
